Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The energy operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 827. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 60 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the energy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 827 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 60. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the energy object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 612. The moduli space has dimension at most 218 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 612 + O(1/n^218). Summing over all modes and applying the energy structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
